data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/CanvasBoard.tsx","content":"import { useRef, useEffect, useState, useCallback, PointerEvent } from 'react';\nimport { CanvasBoardProps } from '../types';\n\ninterface Point {\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n}\n\nconst MAX_HISTORY_STEPS = 20;\n\nconst CanvasBoard = ({\n  settings,\n  onHistoryChange,\n  registerUndo,\n  registerClear,\n  registerDownload,\n}: CanvasBoardProps) => {\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);\n\n  const isDrawingRef = useRef(false);\n  const lastPointRef = useRef<Point | null>(null);\n  const historyRef = useRef<ImageData[]>([]);\n\n  // 最新の settings を描画ハンドラから参照可能にする ref\n  const settingsRef = useRef(settings);\n  settingsRef.current = settings;\n\n  // キャンバスを白で初期化\n  const fillWhiteBackground = (ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, width: number, height: number) => {\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);\n    ctx.restore();\n  };\n\n  // 現在のキャンバス状態を履歴に保存\n  const pushHistory = useCallback(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n\n    try {\n      const imageData = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n      const newHistory = [...historyRef.current, imageData];\n      if (newHistory.length > MAX_HISTORY_STEPS) {\n        newHistory.shift();\n      }\n      historyRef.current = newHistory;\n      onHistoryChange(newHistory.length > 1);\n    } catch {\n      // getImageDataのセキュリティエラー対策\n    }\n  }, [onHistoryChange]);\n\n  // アンドゥ実行\n  const handleUndo = useCallback(() => {\n    if (historyRef.current.length <= 1) return;\n\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n\n    // 現在の状態を破棄\n    historyRef.current.pop();\n    const previousState = historyRef.current[historyRef.current.length - 1];\n\n    if (previousState) {\n      ctx.putImageData(previousState, 0, 0);\n    }\n    onHistoryChange(historyRef.current.length > 1);\n  }, [onHistoryChange]);\n\n  // 全消去実行\n  const handleClear = useCallback(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n\n    fillWhiteBackground(ctx, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n    pushHistory();\n  }, [pushHistory]);\n\n  // 画像ダウンロード実行\n  const handleDownload = useCallback(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n\n    // 白背景で描画された一時オフスクリーンCanvasを作成（念のため透過防止）\n    const exportCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');\n    exportCanvas.width = canvas.width;\n    exportCanvas.height = canvas.height;\n    const exportCtx = exportCanvas.getContext('2d');\n\n    if (!exportCtx) return;\n    exportCtx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n    exportCtx.fillRect(0, 0, exportCanvas.width, exportCanvas.height);\n    exportCtx.drawImage(canvas, 0, 0);\n\n    const dataUrl = exportCanvas.toDataURL('image/png');\n    const link = document.createElement('a');\n    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-').slice(0, 19);\n    link.download = `drawing-${timestamp}.png`;\n    link.href = dataUrl;\n    link.click();\n  }, []);\n\n  // 親コンポーネントへ各関数の登録\n  useEffect(() => {\n    registerUndo(handleUndo);\n  }, [registerUndo, handleUndo]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    registerClear(handleClear);\n  }, [registerClear, handleClear]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    registerDownload(handleDownload);\n  }, [registerDownload, handleDownload]);\n\n  // キャンバスのリサイズ＆高DPI初期化\n  const initializeCanvas = useCallback(() => {\n    const container = containerRef.current;\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!container || !canvas) return;\n\n    const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n    if (rect.width === 0 || rect.height === 0) return;\n\n    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n    const newWidth = Math.floor(rect.width * dpr);\n    const newHeight = Math.floor(rect.height * dpr);\n\n    // 既存の内容がある場合は退避してリサイズ後に転写\n    let tempCanvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;\n    if (canvas.width > 0 && canvas.height > 0) {\n      tempCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');\n      tempCanvas.width = canvas.width;\n      tempCanvas.height = canvas.height;\n      const tempCtx = tempCanvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (tempCtx) {\n        tempCtx.drawImage(canvas, 0, 0);\n      }\n    }\n\n    canvas.width = newWidth;\n    canvas.height = newHeight;\n\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n\n    fillWhiteBackground(ctx, newWidth, newHeight);\n\n    if (tempCanvas) {\n      ctx.drawImage(tempCanvas, 0, 0, newWidth, newHeight);\n    }\n\n    // 初期状態を履歴スタックに登録\n    historyRef.current = [ctx.getImageData(0, 0, newWidth, newHeight)];\n    onHistoryChange(false);\n  }, [onHistoryChange]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    initializeCanvas();\n\n    const handleResize = () => {\n      // ユーザーの描画内容を保持しつつリサイズ\n      const container = containerRef.current;\n      const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      if (!container || !canvas) return;\n\n      const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n      const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n      const newWidth = Math.floor(rect.width * dpr);\n      const newHeight = Math.floor(rect.height * dpr);\n\n      if (canvas.width === newWidth && canvas.height === newHeight) return;\n\n      const tempCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');\n      tempCanvas.width = canvas.width;\n      tempCanvas.height = canvas.height;\n      const tempCtx = tempCanvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (tempCtx) {\n        tempCtx.drawImage(canvas, 0, 0);\n      }\n\n      canvas.width = newWidth;\n      canvas.height = newHeight;\n\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (ctx) {\n        fillWhiteBackground(ctx, newWidth, newHeight);\n        if (tempCanvas.width > 0 && tempCanvas.height > 0) {\n          ctx.drawImage(tempCanvas, 0, 0);\n        }\n        historyRef.current = [ctx.getImageData(0, 0, newWidth, newHeight)];\n        onHistoryChange(false);\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n    };\n  }, [initializeCanvas, onHistoryChange]);\n\n  // ポインター座標の取得（キャンバスピクセル比対応）\n  const getCanvasCoordinates = (e: PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>): Point => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return { x: 0, y: 0 };\n\n    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const scaleX = canvas.width / rect.width;\n    const scaleY = canvas.height / rect.height;\n\n    return {\n      x: (e.clientX - rect.left) * scaleX,\n      y: (e.clientY - rect.top) * scaleY,\n    };\n  };\n\n  // 描画開始\n  const handlePointerDown = (e: PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    // 主ボタン（左クリックまたはタッチ）のみ処理\n    if (e.button !== 0) return;\n\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n\n    canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n    isDrawingRef.current = true;\n\n    const currentPoint = getCanvasCoordinates(e);\n    lastPointRef.current = currentPoint;\n\n    // クリックしたその場にも点滅せず描画（点打ち）\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n\n    const currentSettings = settingsRef.current;\n    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n    const scaledLineWidth = currentSettings.lineWidth * dpr;\n\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.lineCap = 'round';\n    ctx.lineJoin = 'round';\n    ctx.lineWidth = scaledLineWidth;\n\n    if (currentSettings.tool === 'eraser') {\n      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';\n      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n    } else {\n      ctx.strokeStyle = currentSettings.color;\n      ctx.fillStyle = currentSettings.color;\n    }\n\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.arc(currentPoint.x, currentPoint.y, scaledLineWidth / 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n    ctx.fill();\n    ctx.restore();\n  };\n\n  // 描画中（滑らかな補間）\n  const handlePointerMove = (e: PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    if (!isDrawingRef.current || !lastPointRef.current) return;\n\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n\n    const currentPoint = getCanvasCoordinates(e);\n    const lastPoint = lastPointRef.current;\n\n    const currentSettings = settingsRef.current;\n    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n    const scaledLineWidth = currentSettings.lineWidth * dpr;\n\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.lineCap = 'round';\n    ctx.lineJoin = 'round';\n    ctx.lineWidth = scaledLineWidth;\n\n    if (currentSettings.tool === 'eraser') {\n      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';\n    } else {\n      ctx.strokeStyle = currentSettings.color;\n    }\n\n    // 前の座標と現在の座標の中間点を用いたベジェ曲線で滑らかに接続\n    const midPoint = {\n      x: (lastPoint.x + currentPoint.x) / 2,\n      y: (lastPoint.y + currentPoint.y) / 2,\n    };\n\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.moveTo(lastPoint.x, lastPoint.y);\n    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(lastPoint.x, lastPoint.y, midPoint.x, midPoint.y);\n    ctx.lineTo(currentPoint.x, currentPoint.y);\n    ctx.stroke();\n    ctx.restore();\n\n    lastPointRef.current = currentPoint;\n  };\n\n  // 描画終了\n  const handlePointerUp = (e: PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    if (!isDrawingRef.current) return;\n\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (canvas && canvas.hasPointerCapture(e.pointerId)) {\n      try {\n        canvas.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n      } catch {\n        // ignore\n      }\n    }\n\n    isDrawingRef.current = false;\n    lastPointRef.current = null;\n    pushHistory();\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerLeave = (e: PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {\n    // キャプチャ中のポインターはleaveイベントでも追従するため、キャプチャがない場合のみ終了\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (canvas && !canvas.hasPointerCapture(e.pointerId) && isDrawingRef.current) {\n      isDrawingRef.current = false;\n      lastPointRef.current = null;\n      pushHistory();\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <main className=\"canvas-area\">\n      <div className=\"canvas-container\" ref={containerRef}>\n        <canvas\n          ref={canvasRef}\n          className=\"drawing-canvas\"\n          onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}\n          onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}\n          onPointerUp={handlePointerUp}\n          onPointerLeave={handlePointerLeave}\n          onPointerCancel={handlePointerUp}\n        />\n      </div>\n    </main>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default CanvasBoard;","language":"tsx"}

settings, onHistoryChange }, ref) => {\n    const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n    const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);\n\n    const isDrawingRef = useRef(false);\n    const lastPointRef = useRef<Point | null>(null);\n    // 履歴スタックを HTMLCanvasElement (オフスクリーンスナップショット) で保持し、リサイズ時もスケーリング可能にする\n    const historyRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement[]>([]);\n\n    // 最新の settings を描画ハンドラから参照可能にする ref\n    const settingsRef = useRef(settings);\n    settingsRef.current = settings;\n\n    // 現在のキャンバス状態を白背景でなく透過状態（または描画内容）としてスナップショットを作成し保存\n    const pushHistory = useCallback(() => {\n      const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      if (!canvas || canvas.width === 0 || canvas.height === 0) return;\n\n      try {\n        const snapshot = document.createElement('canvas');\n        snapshot.width = canvas.width;\n        snapshot.height = canvas.height;\n        const snapshotCtx = snapshot.getContext('2d');\n        if (!snapshotCtx) return;\n\n        snapshotCtx.drawImage(canvas, 0, 0);\n\n        const newHistory = [...historyRef.current, snapshot];\n        if (newHistory.length > MAX_HISTORY_STEPS) {\n          newHistory.shift();\n        }\n        historyRef.current = newHistory;\n        onHistoryChange(newHistory.length > 1);\n      } catch {\n        // キャンバスキャプチャのセキュリティ等例外防止\n      }\n    }, [onHistoryChange]);\n\n    // アンドゥ実行\n    const handleUndo = useCallback(() => {\n      if (historyRef.current.length <= 1) return;\n\n      const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      if (!canvas) return;\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (!ctx) return;\n\n      // 現在の状態を破棄\n      historyRef.current.pop();\n      const previousSnapshot = const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      if (!container || !canvas) return;\n\n      const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n      if (rect.width === 0 || rect.height === 0) return;\n\n      const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n      const newWidth = Math.floor(rect.width * dpr);\n      const newHeight = Math.floor(rect.height * dpr);\n\n      if (canvas.width === newWidth && canvas.height === newHeight) return;\n\n      const prevWidth = canvas.width;\n      const prevHeight = canvas.height;\n\n      // 最初の初期化時\n      if (prevWidth === 0 || prevHeight === 0) {\n settingsRef.current;\n      const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n      const scaledLineWidth = currentSettings.lineWidth * dpr;\n\n      ctx.save();\n      ctx.lineCap = 'round';\n return;\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (!ctx) return;\n\n      const currentPoint = getCanvasCoordinates(e);\n      const lastPoint = lastPointRef.current;\n\n      const currentSettings = settingsRef.current;\n      const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n      const scaledLineWidth = currentSettings.lineWidth * dpr;\n\n      ctx.save();\n      ctx.lineCap = ctx.quadraticCurveTo(lastPoint.x, lastPoint.y, midPoint.x, midPoint.y);\n      ctx.lineTo(currentPoint.x, currentPoint.y);\n      ctx.stroke();\n      ctx.restore();\n\n      lastPointRef.current =
